## Authentic FFT formatless-write experiment — 3 November 2026

[Original 20-program operation probe 41564682850](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41564682850) COMPLETED SUCCESS on source 59a4a13b. All twenty SHA-authenticated DXBC programs translated to SPIR-V declare StorageImageWriteWithoutFormat capability 56, contain one OpImageWrite and three OpImageFetch per program, and use an Unknown-format storage image. The HLSL/glslang roundtrip instead declares Rgba32f and removes capability 56, with image-operation counts unchanged. No device feature support or numeric output proven.

[Temporary repair source f2507f00](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/commit/f2507f00bf8e7bcbf5aaf312a7b462b9f82aff31), [original program CI 41565279755](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41565279755), job 125685201003, COMPLETED SUCCESS: a fail-closed, private-only experiment restores ImageFormat Unknown and OpCapability56 in each translated output, verifying 20/20 original typed-image declarations and 20/20 Vulkan1.1 spirv-val checks. Modified SPIR-V files exist only in private CI temporary directories and were never staged into game assets or published. This is structural restoration, NOT GPU numerical equivalence, original Unity runtime kernel identity, correct game water, or Adreno feature proof.

[Format classification fix 298faf7a](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/commit/298faf7aa6ad7879dbd9cb0a53e411cce0a9bcae) passed [managed source CI 41564904076](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41564904076). SPIR-V StorageImageExtendedFormats capability is different from Vulkan shaderStorageImageExtendedFormats device feature: RGFloat has Vulkan core-mandatory storage format support, RGHalf may require individual format query when the extended-format guarantee is absent. See [Khronos features specification](https://docs.vulkan.org/spec/latest/chapters/features.html).

NEXT: test genuine numerical FFT GPU/shader outputs and resource-format dependence; correlate runtime kernel mapping and authentic multi-pass ShaderLab programs. Do not fake kernels, toggle strict water gate or claim actual GPU execution. ## Authenticated butterfly storage policy — 3 November 2026

Client raft-20261103T095340Z-a81c6e42 verified original GpuFFT/Dx11FFT SHA-pinned sources and added scripts/probe_fft_butterfly_storage.py. Local execution passed all4 flag combinations;2 unknown-source negative controls rejected. Butterfly storage is RGBAHalf or RGBAFloat selected by highPrecision, independent of twoChannels. Intermediate targets instead select RGHalf/RGFloat or ARGBHalf/ARGBFloat. Original texture setup requests Point/Clamp, linear and no mip chain. Compute override stores integer indices; base GPU path stores normalized centers. Therefore half-coordinate results cannot be generalized across paths or applied when highPrecision selects float. This is an authenticated source-policy mapping, not actual Unity format creation, GPU arithmetic or restored programs. <!-- A4-DIAG010-CALLBACK-RETRY-20261103 -->
## A4 DIAG-010 — reliable repeated-error summary retry (3 November 2026)

[Source 196fce7b](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/commit/196fce7bd147b5baac2bb8214a8c4d0aad5e2af2) fixes a real bookkeeping defect: `RepeatedDiagnosticMessages.Summarize` cleared ALL pending duplicate counts before the first callback; a failing writer could erase unsent groups. The repair snapshots one group immediately before callback outside the lock and restores a failed group's counter alongside incoming concurrent repeats. It preserves first full error stack and bounded 128-key grouping. Rare callbacks that persist then throw can yield at-least-once duplicate summaries on retry.

[Diagnostic tests 41623644272](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41623644272) **COMPLETED SUCCESS**, job125875404710 at exact source 196fce7bd147b5baac2bb8214a8c4d0aad5e2af2: 270 .NET checks, injected IOException and overlapping duplicate tests. [Platform 41623644500](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41623644500) and [original-startup CI 41623644866](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41623644866) also **COMPLETED SUCCESS** on same source. Neither establishes phone logging throughput, Unity/native compilation or an APK, nor fixes game GPU OOM/FFT. A4 scoped identity `raft-20261103T105430Z-a4d9e31b` completed. No CL, LFS, .meta/GUID, left Mouse0/500ms, UGUI/controllers/physical input, native request, Unity/APK/OBB/XAPK/Release or automations altered. ## A1 original FFT arithmetic opcode drift — 3 November 2026

[Code/test source 19884e7757](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/commit/19884e775733feb49763b10b29ee19578dc4790d) adds `scripts/probe_fft_spirv_math_profile.py` with six synthetic regressions and integrates a *numeric-only* arithmetic/conversion/branch/extension instruction census into the private SHA-authenticated FFT DXBC -> vkd3d SPIR-V -> HLSL50 -> glslang SPIR-V audit. [Synthetic no-LFS CI41625193607](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41625193607) **COMPLETED SUCCESS**, job125880661327, the six new tests passed. [Original three-owner CPU audit41625193741](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41625193741) **COMPLETED SUCCESS**, job125880662098, at the exact same source SHA.

20/20 original FFT-name DXBC programs translate and validate for Vulkan1.1; 20/20 roundtrip bindings and workgroups preserved; **0/20 tracked arithmetic/control/extension profiles preserved**, in addition to prior **0/20 typed-image and 0/20 image-operation/capability profile** equality. Aggregate structural counts across 20: `OpBitcast`1050 original ->1435 roundtrip, `OpIAdd`365->318, `OpIMul`205->140, `OpPhi`0->50. Every other tracked numeric arithmetic opcode count matched within each of the 20 pairs; extension-call counts also matched in each pair. ## A7 INPUT secondary raw TouchState routing — verified 3 November 2026

A7 continued independently as `raft-20261103T132738Z-a7f64e28` ([memory](coordination/clients/raft-20261103T132738Z-a7f64e28/MEMORY.md)) after reviewing HEAD, active peer plans, Actions, old APK80 and read-only CL. Exact upstream Unity InputSystem **1.7** `Touchscreen.GetStateOffsetForEvent` restricts `TouchState` control reads to `primaryTouch` (which stays on the first finger), so the previously authored `AndroidTouchPointer.OnEvent` could read the UI/virtual-controller finger in place of a concurrent second gameplay finger. The public 1.7 API `StateEvent.GetState<TouchState>(InputEventPtr)` safely reads each per-finger raw packet.

[Fail-first source-extracted harness e0fda15d](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/commit/e0fda15de3135f241a3e403427dc3f33322d7021) reuses the **actual authored OnEvent** C# method in strict engine doubles with real `TouchPointerGesture`, fake UI hit-test and per-finger vs primary event payloads. [CPU-only red CI41645203051](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41645203051), job125948599849, **FAILED as expected** with `MULTITOUCH_ROUTE: second-gameplay-touch-recognized`. [Source fix 583f414d](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/commit/583f414dee85f1659783a08ab2b4363cc2992d14) reads touch-specific id/position/phase via `StateEvent.GetState<TouchState>`, rejects invalid zero IDs, and retains the prior `primaryTouch` fallback for non-TouchState full-state events. UI ownership, virtual Xbox, actual packet `handled` logic, LEFT Mouse0,500ms and physical-device paths are otherwise untouched.

[Green CPU platform41645428990](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41645428990), job125949388401 **COMPLETED SUCCESS** on exact repaired source: **14** new raw routing checks (first UI+second gameplay, inverse order, sticky UGUI, fallback, paused/handled), plus **446** gesture checks, **53** Mouse0 mapping checks and15 ancillary suites. [Offline original managed41645429004](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41645429004), job125949389297 **SUCCESS** `RAFT_OFFLINE_COMPILE exit=0 errorCodes={} affectedFiles=[]`. **Limits:** no real Unity InputSystem or phone execution, UGUI hit test mocked, old installed APK80 predates the patch. This is a source fix and CPU behavioral test only; general input/device acceptance remains OPEN. No authentic water/shader or world blocker closed; **NOT_READY**, no final immutable candidate, triple readiness reviews **0/3**. No Unity/APK/OBB/XAPK build, Release, LFS, CL, protected branch or scheduling changes.

## A3 DELIVERY-013 — release package atomic reassembly (3 November 2026)

A3 resumed as `raft-20261103T145149Z-a3e71b29` and repaired an actual POSIX output-overwrite race in the copied user-facing `reassemble.py` release helper. The previous `scripts/reassemble_android_delivery.py` verified part and whole-result SHA-256, checked that the final .apk/.obb/.xapk path did not exist, then used `Path.rename`; another assembler could create the final file after that check and be silently overwritten. Synthetic fail-first [CI41658379104](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41658379104) deliberately **FAILED 1/4** at `ValueError not raised` for this real reassembler race.

[Source fix c568e6c6](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/commit/c568e6c6c090c2ade8d15f1c5d70d061ba66940e) atomically publishes the fully SHA-verified temporary by exclusive `os.link` and deletes only the owned temporary on success, without a second multi-GB copy; hard-link `FileExistsError` now refuses to clobber the competing file. Filesystems such as Android shared-storage mounts may deny links; this condition **fails safely** with an explicit **reassemble inside Termux home** message rather than falling back to unsafe `rename`. No real phone storage support is claimed.

[GREEN CI41658736942](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41658736942) **SUCCESS** on source fix (4 new + 13 existing delivery tests). An additional unsupported-filesystem negative control [test8f19c687](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/commit/8f19c687eaef96683042359583ac6272555bbe63) verified no published partial file on injected hard-link failure: final [CI41658894880](https://github.com/alwansan/raf/actions/runs/41658894880), job125996555701, **COMPLETED SUCCESS 5/5 new +13/13 existing** including ZIP64 XAPK byte-for-byte delivery and sha256 checks. [A3 full report](../docs/A3_RELEASE_REASSEMBLY_ATOMIC_2026-11-03.md); task state/experiment/discussion closed in its new client path.

The game itself was **not built or released**, old APK80 is not a new native test, and this change does not address authentic WATER-001/RENDER-002/FFT-FORMAT-009 shader/kernel recovery, world, graphics or on-phone input. **NOT_READY, no fixed candidate, three full independent immutable source reviews 0/3**. Preferred original input: https://github.com/alwansan/raf/tree/zip2/Raft
Reviewed immutable source commit: 15964ea8f246b6e076ce26b60931ee2e1a9a2385.
Read zip2 only; write source/code/docs only on feature/android-native-port. Protected main/zip/Unity-Editor unchanged.

## Verified inventory and limits
RAFT_FILE_MANIFEST.tsv has366 original files,7789295609 bytes,22 split files. Recursive Git tree is complete (577 entries); all plain file sizes and all split-part summed sizes match manifest. resources.assets manifest SHA87493249f1adfc0c5eb8b82e4e199e208cd2c95eabe9796cab82116c4838595c matches existing authenticated source pin.
This is structural verification, NOT full7.79GB content SHA verification or successful Unity import. Every restored file must pass original size/SHA before use.

## Use
Fetch pinned zip2 into an isolated checkout with LFS smudge disabled, without switching or overwriting another client's worktree. Read README_ZIP2.txt, RAFT_FILE_MANIFEST.tsv and restore_raft.sh. Run bash restore_raft.sh /absolute/separate/Raft-restored in that checkout. Output must be separate from source and existing projects. Restore only needed providers if bandwidth/disk matters; concatenate part-00000 etc in exact order, verify final size and SHA, then stage under original path. Keep metadata and source hash checks. Never pass split folders as .assets/.resS/.bank files or execute Windows binaries.

Prefer ZIP2 verified reconstruction over new git lfs pull. Reuse already verified local/cache payloads. Do not silently fall back to LFS or claim unavailable payload success. Existing workflows still using LFS require a deliberate adapter/checkout/staging change; this documentation does not convert them automatically. Pin source SHA in experiment records and compare existing original fingerprints; investigate mismatches.

## Migration choice
Keep feature/android-native-port/Raft for now: existing pointer identity and Git blob validators rely on it. Do not copy7.79GB/split trees over it or delete it wholesale. ZIP2 is the external original-data provider; stage restored files in disposable experiment directories. Gradually adapt consumers while retaining original authenticity checks, .meta/GUIDs, scene/material links, kernel ordering and all content.

## All clients and tasks
A1–A15, W1/W2/W3 and recovery helpers: read this notice before further original-data acquisition. Communicate through coordination records; no immediate cross-session delivery is implied. Prioritize substantive water/FFT/shader/color/material/effect restoration and data recovery. Do not spend cycles on repetitive audits when original source progress is possible. Preserve each role's Unity/build restrictions and ongoing jobs. Successful reconstruction does not close rendering/audio/world/device gates.
